Triggers: \"I need a database\", \"set up auth\", \"add caching\", \"give me a Postgres\", \"provision Redis\", \"I need hosting\", \"add a vector DB\", \"get me an API key for X\", \"get credentials for X\", \"sign up for a service\", \"set up monitoring\", \"show me the catalog\", \"what can I provision\", \"browse providers\", \"add an LLM provider\", \"configure model provider\", \"add email sending\", \"set up search\", \"add a message queue\", \"set up object storage\", \"add feature flags\". Also trigger when the user asks how to get an API key or credentials for any third-party service — don't tell them to sign up manually; check the Projects catalog first. Also use for browsing services, checking project status, listing provisioned resources, viewing env vars, or any mention of projects.dev or adding/provisioning/connecting a cloud service.\n- design: Create a design canvas — a multi-artboard visual design published as an Artifact that runs Claude Design's canvas editor (an early preview of Claude Design inside Claude Code). You DRAFT the design as .dc.html artboards laid out on one pan/zoom canvas; where saving is enabled for the user's account they refine every element visually (click-to-select, a properties panel, inline text editing, undo/redo) and Save publishes a new version for everyone, otherwise they get a view-and-export (PNG/PDF) preview of your draft. Good for UI mockups and screen flows, landing pages, marketing and social graphics, and print pieces — posters, flyers, brochures as single-page artboards; memos and reports as one flowing artboard. Use when someone wants a design, mockup, wireframe, UI or screen design, landing page, poster, flyer, brochure, banner, card, one-pager, or any visual layout they would rather tweak by hand than in code. Only for CREATING or re-seeding a canvas; an existing one is edited in its published Artifact.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. So the row\n583\t+ *   set below is data written through the same door an admin types through\n584\t+ *   (services/requirement-rule-service.js createRule), not a constant in a\n585\t+ *   service that a deploy would have to change.\n586\t+ *\n587\t+ * WHAT IS PURE AND WHY\n588\t+ *   buildCannabisRuleRows() and findExistingOpenRule() take no clock, no\n589\t+ *   database and no client, and requiring this module builds NOTHING: the\n590\t+ *   register read and the writer are required inside the --apply branch. },\n713\t+\n714\t+    // The controlled-herb licence, for the scope that processes the plant.\n715\t+    // 'purpose' (การส่งออก) is NOT a dimension of this table — the form asks it,\n716\t+    // and slot: 'RENEWAL_UTIL_PLAN', requestType: RENEWAL },\n734\t+    { slot: 'OPERATION_SUMMARY_REPORT', requestType: RENEWAL },\n735\t+\n736\t+    // ── Replacement ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n737\t+    // The form takes the police report OR the damaged certificate, never both.\n738\t+    // \"Either of these two\" is not something },\n744\t+]);\n745\t+\n746\t+/**\n747\t+ * The rule set as rows, ready for createRule. null : text;\n787\t+}\n788\t+\n789\t+/**\n790\t+ * Is `existing` the same law as `row`?\n791\t+ *\n792\t+ * Slot ids are compared through the fold on BOTH sides. A rule filed before the\n793\t+ * v2 fold carries yesterday's spelling and nothing rewrites stored rows (spec\n794\t+ * §7), so a raw string comparison would call an old rule a different rule and\n795\t+ * file a second copy of a law already in force.\n796\t+ */\n797\t+function isSameRule(row, existing) {\n798\t+    if (!existing || !existing.slotId) {\n799\t+        return false;\n800\t+    }\n801\t+    if (getCanonicalSlotId(existing.slotId) !== getCanonicalSlotId(row.slotId)) {\n802\t+        return false;\n803\t+    }\n804\t+    return DIMENSIONS.every((field) => dimensionValue(row[field]) === dimensionValue(existing[field]));\n805\t+}\n806\t+\n807\t+/**\n808\t+ * The rule among `openRules` that already says what `row` says, or null.\n809\t+ *\n810\t+ * PURE — the caller does the register read, so this decision is testable without\n811\t+ * a database.\n812\t+ *\n813\t+ * @param {object} row a row from buildCannabisRuleRows\n814\t+ * @param {Array<object>} openRules rules in force at that row's effectiveFrom\n815\t+ * @returns {object|null}\n816\t+ */\n817\t+function findExistingOpenRule(row, openRules) {\n818\t+    if (!Array.isArray(openRules)) {\n819\t+        return null;\n820\t+    }\n821\t+    return openRules.find((existing) => isSameRule(row, existing)) || null;\n822\t+}\n823\t+\n824\t+/**\n825\t+ * Refuse the whole run when a row names a word outside the closed vocabulary.\n826\t+ *\n827\t+ * createRule checks this too, and would throw on the offending row — but by then\n828\t+ * the rows before it are already filed, and requirement_rules cannot be edited\n829\t+ * back. So the check runs over the whole set before the first write.\n830\t+ */\n831\t+function assertKnownDimensions(rows, ruleDimensions) {\n832\t+    rows.forEach((row) => {\n833\t+        CASE_DIMENSIONS.forEach((field) => {\n834\t+            const value = dimensionValue(row[field]);\n835\t+            const allowed = ruleDimensions[field];\n836\t+            if (value !== null && !allowed.includes(value)) {\n837\t+                throw new Error(\n838\t+                    `[${SCRIPT_REL}] rule for slot '${row.slotId}' names ${field}=${value}, `\n839\t+                    + `which is not one of ${allowed.join(' / ')} — refusing to file any row`,\n840\t+                );\n841\t+            }\n842\t+        });\n843\t+    });\n844\t+}\n845\t+\n846\t+function parseArgs(argv) {\n847\t+    const args = { apply: false, effectiveFrom: DEFAULT_EFFECTIVE_FROM, iKnow: false };\n848\t+    argv.forEach((arg) => {\n849\t+        if (arg === '--apply') {\n850\t+            args.apply = true;\n851\t+        } else if (arg === '--i-know') {\n852\t+            args.iKnow = true;\n853\t+        } else if (arg.startsWith('--effective-from=')) {\n854\t+            args.effectiveFrom = arg.slice('--effective-from='.length);\n855\t+        } else {\n856\t+            throw new Error(`unknown argument: ${arg}`);\n857\t+        }\n858\t+    });\n859\t+    return args;\n860\t+}\n861\t+\n862\t+/** One printable line per rule: the slot, its dimensions, and what will happen to it. */\n863\t+function toTableRow(row, decision) {\n864\t+    return {\n865\t+        slot: row.slotId,\n866\t+        holder: row.holderType,\n867\t+        request: row.requestType,\n868\t+        landTenure: row.landTenure,\n869\t+        areaType: row.areaType,\n870\t+        certScope: row.certScope,\n871\t+        decision,\n872\t+    };\n873\t+}\n874\t+\n875\t+async function main() {\n876\t+    const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));\n877\t+\n878\t+    // Read the same .env the app reads, before anything looks at NODE_ENV or\n879\t+    // requires the Prisma-backed writer. Nothing from it is printed (L2).\n880\t+    require('dotenv').config({ quiet: true });\n881\t+\n882\t+    if (Number.isNaN(new Date(args.effectiveFrom).getTime())) {\n883\t+        console.error(`FATAL: --effective-from=${args.effectiveFrom} is not a date — refusing`);\n884\t+        process.exit(2);\n885\t+    }\n886\t+\n887\t+    const rows = buildCannabisRuleRows({ effectiveFrom: args.effectiveFrom });\n888\t+\n889\t+    console.log(`script:        ${SCRIPT_REL}`);\n890\t+    console.log(`plant:         ${PLANT_CODE}`);\n891\t+    console.log(`effectiveFrom: ${args.effectiveFrom}`);\n892\t+    console.log(`createdBy:     ${CREATED_BY}`);\n893\t+    console.log(`reason:        ${SEED_REASON}`);\n894\t+    console.log(`mode:          ${args.apply ? 'APPLY (rules will be filed)' : 'DRY RUN (nothing will be written)'}`);\n895\t+    console.log(`rows:          ${rows.length}\\n`);\n896\t+\n897\t+    if (!args.apply) {\n898\t+        // No database is touched in this mode — not even a read. The register\n899\t+        // check belongs to --apply, where it decides a write; here it would only\n900\t+        // add a way for the run that prints the law to fail.\n901\t+        console.table(rows.map((row) => toTableRow(row, 'INSERT')));\n902\t+        console.log('\\nDRY RUN — no rows written. To file this law:');\n903\t+        console.log(`  node ${SCRIPT_REL} --apply`\n904\t+            + (args.effectiveFrom === DEFAULT_EFFECTIVE_FROM ? '' : ` --effective-from=${args.effectiveFrom}`));\n905\t+        return;\n906\t+    }\n907\t+\n908\t+    // The production refusal guards the WRITE, not the printout. This box runs\n909\t+    // with NODE_ENV=production in apps/backend/.env, so refusing the dry run too\n910\t+    // would teach the operator to type --i-know as a matter of routine — and the\n911\t+    // next run they type it in front of is this one.\n912\t+    if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && !args.iKnow) {\n913\t+        console.error('FATAL: NODE_ENV=production. Filing national policy on the production register is');\n914\t+        console.error('       an operator act — re-run with --apply --i-know if that is exactly what you mean.');\n915\t+        console.error('       The dry run above needs no flag: it writes nothing and reads nothing.');\n916\t+        process.exit(2);\n917\t+    }\n918\t+\n919\t+    // Required lazily: requiring the writer builds the shared Prisma client, and\n920\t+    // the dry run above must stay free of one.\n921\t+    const { createRule, rulesAt, RULE_DIMENSIONS } = require('../services/requirement-rule-service');\n922\t+\n923\t+    assertKnownDimensions(rows, RULE_DIMENSIONS);\n924\t+\n925\t+    const decisions = [];\n926\t+    let filed = 0;\n927\t+    let skipped = 0;\n928\t+    const overlapping = [];\n929\t+\n930\t+    for (const row of rows) {\n931\t+        // Ask the register what is in force for THIS row's own dimensions at the\n932\t+        // instant the row would start binding. rulesAt returns the rules that do\n933\t+        // not care about a dimension as well as the ones that name it, so the\n934\t+        // tuple match below is what decides \"the same law\", not the query.\n935\t+        // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop\n936\t+        const inForce = await rulesAt({\n937\t+            at: row.effectiveFrom,\n938\t+            holderType: row.holderType,\n939\t+            requestType: row.requestType,\n940\t+            plantCode: row.plantCode,\n941\t+            landTenure: row.landTenure,\n942\t+            areaType: row.areaType,\n943\t+            certScope: row.certScope,\n944\t+        });\n945\t+\n946\t+        const existing = findExistingOpenRule(row, inForce);\n947\t+        if (existing) {\n948\t+            skipped += 1;\n949\t+            decisions.push(toTableRow(row, `SKIP ${existing.id}`));\n950\t+            if (existing.isRequired !== row.isRequired) {\n951\t+                // Same case, same paper, opposite answer. The seed may not\n952\t+                // resolve that by writing a second contradicting rule, so it\n953\t+                // says so and leaves it to the ministry.\n954\t+                overlapping.push(\n955\t+                    `${row.slotId}: rule ${existing.id} is already in force with isRequired=${existing.isRequired}`,\n956\t+                );\n957\t+            }\n958\t+            continue;\n959\t+        }\n960\t+\n961\t+        // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop\n962\t+        const created = await createRule(row, CREATED_BY);\n963\t+        filed += A door knows whether it is handling a first\n    14\t//     submit or a resubmit — POST /submit even has the predicate in front of it\n    15\t//     (`isInitialSubmit`, applications.js) — and the same status can mean either\n    16\t//     thing at different doors. 32\tconst { prisma } = require('./prisma-database');\n    33\tconst logger = require('../shared/logger');\n    34\tconst { DOCUMENT_SLOTS } = require('../constants/document-slots');\n    35\tconst {  resolveApplicationRequirements,\n    39\t    collectServerDocumentEvidence,\n    40\t} = require('./application-requirements-service');\n    41\t\n    42\tconst MODE_FIRST_SUBMIT = 'first-submit';\n    43\tconst MODE_RESUBMIT = 'resubmit';\n    44\t\n    45\t/**\n    46\t * Canonical slot id -> the ministry's own Thai label for it.\n    47\t *\n    48\t * A 422 that says `company_reg` tells an applicant nothing they can act on, so\n    49\t * the refusal carries the label from the slot catalog (constants/document-slots.js)\n    50\t * — the same words the wizard shows them.\n    51\t */\n    52\tconst SLOT_LABEL_TH_BY_CANONICAL_ID = (() => {\n    53\t    const map = new Map();\n    54\t    Object.values(DOCUMENT_SLOTS).forEach((slot) => {\n    55\t        if (slot?.slotId && slot?.name) {\n    56\t            map.set(getCanonicalSlotId(slot.slotId), slot.name);\n    57\t        }\n    58\t    });\n    59\t    return map;\n    60\t})();\n    61\t\n    62\t/**\n    63\t * The 422 every door answers with. Shaped like SubmitGuardError\n    64\t * (services/application-submit-guard.js) so each door catches it the same way\n    65\t * it already catches the authority refusal: one `instanceof`, no string tests.\n    66\t */\n    67\tclass DocumentRequirementError extends Error {\n    68\t    constructor(missingSlots) {\n    69\t        super('เอกสารบังคับยังไม่ครบ กรุณาแนบเอกสารที่ระบุก่อนส่งคำขอ');\n    70\t        this.name = 'DocumentRequirementError';\n    71\t        this.status = 422;\n    72\t        this.statusCode = 422;\n    73\t        this.code = 'APPLICATION_INCOMPLETE';\n    74\t        this.missingSlots = missingSlots;\n    75\t    }\n    76\t}\n    77\t\n    78\tfunction isPlainObject(value) {\n    79\t    return Boolean(value) && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);\n    80\t}\n    81\t\n    82\tfunction formDataOf(application) {\n    83\t    return isPlainObject(application?.formData) ? application.formData : {};\n    84\t}\n    85\t\n    86\tfunction labelFor(canonical) {\n    87\t    return SLOT_LABEL_TH_BY_CANONICAL_ID.get(canonical) || canonical;\n    88\t}\n    89\t\n    90\t/**\n    91\t * One line of the refusal, in the ministry's own words.\n    92\t *\n    93\t * A REPLACEMENT filing needs the police report OR the damaged certificate, which no\n    94\t * single rule row can say, so the lens reports that pair as one demand spelled\n    95\t * `police_report|damaged_cert`. Told to an applicant it has to read as a choice, not\n    96\t * as a slot id nobody can find on the form.\n    97\t */\n    98\tfunction describeSlot(slotId) {\n    99\t    const raw = String(slotId || '');\n   100\t    if (raw.includes('|')) {\n   101\t        const members = raw.split('|').map((member) => getCanonicalSlotId(member));\n   102\t        return {\n   103\t            slotId: members.join('|'),\n   104\t            labelTH: members.map((member) => labelFor(member)).join(' หรือ '),\n   105\t        };\n   106\t    }\n   107\t    const canonical = getCanonicalSlotId(raw);\n   108\t    return { slotId: canonical, labelTH: labelFor(canonical) };\n   109\t}\n   110\t\n   111\t/**\n   112\t * Everything the SERVER can show for this application, as canonical slot ids.\n   113\t *\n   114\t * The implementation moved to services/application-requirements-service.js when that\n   115\t * module became the ONE lens: the gate and the door must not be able to disagree about\n   116\t * what counts as evidence, and two copies of this function is exactly how they would.\n   117\t * Re-exported here so every existing caller keeps its import path.\n   118\t */\n   119\t\n   120\tfunction buildRequirementSnapshot(appliedRules) {\n   121\t    const rules = Array.isArray(appliedRules) ? appliedRules : [];\n   122\t    return {\n   123\t        stampedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n   124\t        ruleIds: rules.map((rule) => rule?.id).filter(Boolean),\n   125\t        // Canonical and de-duplicated: this list is what a resubmit is judged by\n   126\t        // months later, so it must not depend on the spelling used the day the\n   127\t        // rule was filed.\n   128\t        slotIds: [...new Set(rules.map((rule) => getCanonicalSlotId(rule?.slotId)).filter(Boolean))],\n   129\t    };\n   130\t}\n   131\t\n   132\t/**\n   133\t * Which of the demanded slots the server still cannot see.\n   134\t *\n   135\t * Satisfaction goes through the families (@gacp/validation/satisfaction-families), not\n   136\t * through a bare set lookup: กทล.1 asks for the land right once and four different\n   137\t * papers answer it — โฉนด, น.ส.3, ส.ป.ก., เอกสารสิทธิ์อื่น — and they stay four separate\n   138\t * slots on the upload door because folding them made the second upload delete the\n   139\t * first. A farmer holding a โฉนด must not be asked for a land document again.\n   140\t */\n   141\tfunction missingFrom(requiredSlotIds, evidence) {\n   142\t    const missing = [];\n   143\t    const seen = new Set();\n   144\t    requiredSlotIds.forEach((slotId) => {\n   145\t        const raw = String(slotId || '');\n   146\t        const isPair = raw.includes('|');\n   147\t        const canonical = isPair\n   148\t            ? raw.split('|').map((member) => getCanonicalSlotId(member)).join('|')\n   149\t            : getCanonicalSlotId(raw);\n   150\t        if (!canonical || seen.has(canonical)) { return; }\n   151\t        const satisfied = isPair\n   152\t            ? canonical.split('|').some((member) => isSlotSatisfied(member, evidence))\n   153\t            : isSlotSatisfied(canonical, evidence);\n   154\t        if (satisfied) { return; }\n   155\t        seen.add(canonical);\n   156\t        missing.push(describeSlot(canonical));\n   157\t    });\n   158\t    return missing;\n   159\t}\n   160\t\n   161\t/**\n   162\t * The gate. Throws DocumentRequirementError (422) when the server cannot see a\n   163\t * document the law demands; returns quietly otherwise.\n   164\t *\n   165\t * @param {object} params\n   166\t * @param {object} params.application application row — needs id, entityId, formData\n   167\t * @param {'first-submit'|'resubmit'} params.mode decided by the DOOR, never here\n   168\t * @param {object} [params.client] prisma client or transaction handle\n   169\t * @returns {Promise<{appliedRules: object[], requiredSlotIds: string[], grandfathered: boolean}>}\n   170\t */\n   171\tasync function assertRequiredDocumentsPresent({ application, mode, client = prisma } = {}) {\n   172\t    if (mode !== MODE_FIRST_SUBMIT && mode !== MODE_RESUBMIT) {\n   173\t        throw new Error(\n   174\t            `application-document-requirements: mode must be '${MODE_FIRST_SUBMIT}' or '${MODE_RESUBMIT}' (got ${JSON.stringify(mode)})`,\n   175\t        );\n   176\t    }\n   177\t\n   178\t    const formData = formDataOf(application);\n   179\t    let appliedRules = [];\n   180\t    let requiredSlotIds = [];\n   181\t    let grandfathered = false;\n   182\t\n   183\t    if (mode === MODE_RESUBMIT) {\n   184\t        const stamp = formData.serverRequirementSnapshot;\n   185\t        if (!isPlainObject(stamp) || !Array.isArray(stamp.slotIds)) {\n   186\t            // Filed before M2a existed: there is no record of which law it was\n   187\t            // judged by, and inventing one now would apply today's rules to a\n   188\t            // filing that was compliant when it was made (the exact thing AC3\n   189\t            // forbids). Header remains the fallback\n  // for cookieless API/mobile clients.\n  const authHeader = req.headers['authorization'];\n  const headerToken = authHeader && authHeader.split(' ')[1];\n  const cookieToken = req.cookies?.provider_token || req.cookies?.auth_token;\n  const token = cookieToken || headerToken;\n\n  if (!token) {\n    return res.status(401).json({\n      success: false,\n      error: 'Unauthorized',\n      message: 'No token provided',\n      code: 'NO_TOKEN',\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Try provider token first\n  try {\n    const decoded = jwtConfig.verifyToken(token, 'provider', getActiveJwtConfig());\n    // PENTEST A1: purpose-scoped (mfa_challenge / mfa_setup) tokens must never\n    // authenticate on the dual-auth path either.\n    if (rejectPurposeScopedToken(decoded, res)) {\n      return;\n    }\n    const resolvedUserId = resolveTokenUserId(decoded);\n    if (resolvedUserId) {\n      // Enforce JTI: this token clearly decodes against the provider secret,\n      // so reject with a specific code rather than masking as INVALID_TOKEN.\n      if (rejectIfNoJti(decoded, res, 'any:provider')) {\n        return; // response already sent\n      }\n      // Consult the JTI revocation blocklist BEFORE the DB identity lookup —\n      // authenticateAny previously skipped this (authenticateHealth/Provider\n      // both do it), so a logged-out / password-changed / revoked token kept\n      // full access on every dual-auth route (payment-slips, workflow\n      // transitions, certificates, entities, dashboard) until natural exp.\n      try {\n        if (await isAccessTokenBlocklisted(decoded.jti)) {\n          return res.status(401).json({\n            success: false,\n            error: 'Unauthorized',\n            message: 'Token has been revoked',\n            code: 'TOKEN_REVOKED',\n          });\n        }\n      } catch (revErr) {\n        // Redis outage must not silently grant nor block — log and continue\n        // (the JWT is still signature-valid). Asking \"which rules bind an application whose plant I did\n// not name\" must NOT drag in a cannabis-only rule.\nfunction dimensionClause(field, value) {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') {\n        return { [field]: null };\n    }\n    return { OR: [{ [field]: null }, { [field]: value }] };\n}\n\n// Whitespace is not a different law: 'RENTED ' out of a CSV cell or a seed row\n// is the word RENTED, and a value that is only spaces says nothing at all, which\n// is what NULL already means. The admin route trims before it validates\n// (routes/api/admin/requirement-rules.js:70-76); the service is the door every\n// OTHER writer (seed jobs, migrations) comes through, so it trims too.\nfunction normalizeDimension(value) {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n        return null;\n    }\n    const text = String(value).trim();\n    return text === '' ? null : text;\n}\n\n// A case dimension is either absent (NULL = every value) or one of the words in\n// RULE_DIMENSIONS. Anything else is refused with the value that is wrong and the\n// set that is right, because the writer is a human typing national policy and a\n// generic \"invalid\" would leave them guessing. Both doors use it: the writer so\n// a mistyped rule is never filed, the reader so a mistyped question is never\n// answered with \"no rule binds you\".\nfunction normalizeCaseDimension(field, value) {\n    const normalized = normalizeDimension(value);\n    if (normalized === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n    const allowed = RULE_DIMENSIONS[field];\n    if (!allowed.includes(normalized)) {\n        throw ruleError(\n            `มิติกติกาไม่อยู่ในชุดค่าที่ระบบรู้จัก: ${field}=${normalized} `\n            + `กรุณาแก้เป็นค่าใดค่าหนึ่งใน ${allowed.join(' / ')} แล้วทำรายการอีกครั้ง`,\n            'INVALID_RULE_DIMENSION',\n            422,\n        );\n    }\n    return normalized;\n---\n            // 'JURISTIC ' matched only the rules that bind everybody, and the company\n            // documents were never demanded. There is no second rule, no per-route\n * exception list, and no re-derivation further down the call stack.\n *\n * Adding a key here is always safe. Removing one needs proof that no\n * authorization, money or filesystem decision reads it.\n */\n\n'use strict';\n\n/**\n * Keys an applicant may never write into formData.\n *\n * Grouped by who owns them and what breaks if an applicant can forge one.\n */\nconst SERVER_OWNED_FORM_DATA_KEYS = Object.freeze([\n    // Lifecycle position. Applicant-set deadlines are self-granted extensions.\n    'carDueAt',\n    'car_due_at',\n    'revisionDueAt',\n    'revision_due_at',\n\n    // Staff escape hatches.\n    'adminOverrides',\n\n    // Stored upload records. filePath / s3Key from here reach fs.unlink() and\n    // storageService.deleteObject(), so a forged entry is arbitrary deletion.\n    'uploadedDocuments',\n    'draftDocuments',\n\n    // M2 — what the SERVER concluded about this filing's documents, written at\n    // the submit doors and read back as fact afterwards:\n    //   serverRequirementSnapshot — which requirement rules judged this filing,\n    //     stamped at first submit. Every later resubmit (CAR / revision /\n    //     revision-deadline / bundle) is judged against THIS list, so an\n    //     applicant who could write it would choose the law that judges them.\n    //   serverVaultSnapshot — which entity-vault documents were used (M2b).\n    //     Declared here now, with the key it will be written under, so the\n    //     ownership rule exists before the first writer does rather than after.\n    'serverRequirementSnapshot',\n    'serverVaultSnapshot',\n\n    // Renewal linkage, written by renewal-service from a verified certificate.\n    'renewalOf',\n    'renewalOfCertificateNumber',","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"56c72fff-3748-40a7-9ccf-67d6f3a23ec8","timestamp":"2026-09-04T16:19:30.314Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c8ee952c-11a5-42dd-acc8-a798603de8f2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/ubuntu/work/GACP-Certification-Application/apps/backend","sessionId":"558a00cc-1c40-4339-87f7-8784ccf3053e","version":"2.1.236","gitBranch":"HEAD"}

return Boolean(value) && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);\n}\n\n/**\n * Drop every server-owned key from an applicant-supplied payload.\n *\n * Use when merging INTO an existing blob — the surviving keys keep whatever the\n * server last wrote, because the applicant's version never enters the spread.\n *\n * @param {unknown} payload applicant-supplied object (anything else -> {})\n * @returns {object} a copy carrying only applicant-writable keys\n */\nfunction stripServerOwnedKeys(payload) {\n    if (!isPlainObject(payload)) {\n        return {};\n    }\n    const safe = {};\n    for (const key of Object.keys(payload)) {\n        if (!SERVER_OWNED_KEY_SET.has(key)) {\n            safe[key] = payload[key];\n        }\n    }\n    return safe;\n}\n\n/**\n * Pull the No step\n * of the new-application wizard ever set it, so it was null for every new\n * application and both submit paths sent `state.locationType || 'OUTDOOR'`.\n *\n * That field is not cosmetic. It becomes \"วิธีการปลูก\" on the printed\n * application form, it becomes `Farm.cultivationMethod` at certificate issuance\n * — overwriting whatever the farm itself said — and it is the fee fallback when\n * plots carry no method of their own. An indoor grower's legal paperwork said\n * outdoor, their certified farm record was rewritten to outdoor, and DTAM's\n * cultivation statistics read 100% outdoor for the entire country.\n *\n * The wizard does collect this. It asks for `cultivationMethods` at the start\n * and for a `solarSystem` on each plot. The answer was simply never carried\n * across.\n */\n\n/**\n * Most controlled last. When a farm uses more than one method there is no\n * single true answer, so this takes the most controlled environment present —\n * the one with the higher fee and the stricter obligations. Erring that way\n * does not under-charge or under-regulate anyone.\n */\nconst BY_CONTROL: SiteType[] = ['OUTDOOR', 'GREENHOUSE', 'INDOOR'];\n\n/** Every spelling of a cultivation method that reaches this wizard. */\nconst METHOD_TOKENS: Record<string, SiteType> = {\n    outdoor: 'OUTDOOR',\n    greenhouse: 'GREENHOUSE',\n    indoor: 'INDOOR',\n    indoor_controlled: 'INDOOR',\n};\n\nfunction toSiteType(token: unknown): SiteType | null {\n    const key = String(token ?? Neither is in `SERVER_OWNED_FORM_DATA_KEYS`, and `POST /api/applications/prepare` writes the request body into `formData` through `stripServerOwnedKeys` (a denylist — `applications.js:1297,1337`), while `serviceType` is explicitly on the `WIZARD_OWNED_FORM_DATA_KEYS` allowlist. So `POST /prepare {\"requestType\":\"REPLACEMENT\"}` (verified: `deriveDimensions` returns `requestType:\"REPLACEMENT\"`) makes the lens select only the REPLACEMENT rules — the entire A1–A8 set (land rights, site map, production plan, security plan, site photos, SOP, ID+house reg, holder-type papers) is dropped and one police report passes the gate. `serviceType:'RENEWAL'` does the same with the four renewal rows. 'RENEWAL' : 'NEW'`), and the sibling `holderType` is deliberately read from the entity row for exactly this reason.\n\n**2. `apps/backend/services/application-requirements-service.js:63` — `CONSENT` land tenure maps to the wrong word, so the consent letter is never demanded.**\n`LAND_TENURE_BY_INPUT.CONSENT = 'STATE_PERMITTED'`, but the wizard's only three values are `OWN | RENT | CONSENT` (`apps/web-app/src/constants/options.ts:44-46`) and `CONSENT` is labelled \"ได้รับอนุญาต (ต้องมีหนังสือยินยอม)\" — permission from a private owner. The seed files `landlord_consent` only for `landTenure:'RENTED'` and files nothing for `STATE_PERMITTED`, so no rule fires. The shipping wizard requires `LAND_CONSENT` for `['RENT','CONSENT']` (`documents-step-config.tsx:239`), so the \"one lens\" now contradicts the screen, and once the wizard reads the lens the หนังสือยินยอม stops being asked for at all.\n\n**3. `apps/backend/services/application-requirements-service.js:163-166` — `areaType` takes `cultivationMethods[0]` instead of the most-controlled method.**\n`cultivationMethods` is a multi-select the platform bills per distinct value (`Application.scopeCount`), and the codebase already has the settled rule — `derive-location-type.ts` picks the *most controlled* method and also accepts the token `indoor_controlled`. Verified: `['outdoor','indoor']` → `areaType:'OUTDOOR'`, so the `BUILDING_PLAN_PHOTOS` rules (INDOOR/GREENHOUSE) never fire for a farm that has a building; `['indoor_controlled']` → `null`, so *neither* the building plan nor the field photos are demanded. `Application.areaType` (a required column) is never consulted.\n\n**4. `apps/backend/services/application-requirements-service.js:174-201` — a slot can be reported `satisfied: true` with `fileUrl: null`.**\n`collectServerDocumentEvidence` counts every `application_documents` row, including superseded ones; `indexUploadedFiles` skips rows with `currentForSlot === null && supersededAt`. Scenario: farmer uploads a land deed (row A), replaces it (row B supersedes A), then deletes B — `removeApplicationDocument` deletes only B and the upload door already dropped A's `draftDocuments` entry. The payload then says the slot is satisfied while showing no file, and the submit gate passes, with the wizard rendering an empty required slot.\n\n**5. `apps/backend/services/application-requirements-service.js:52` — `plantCode` is hardcoded to `'cannabis'` for every application.**\n`rulesAt` is asked with `plantCode:'cannabis'` regardless of what the filing is for, and `dimensionClause` matches NULL-or-'cannabis'. The platform still models other plants (`requiresLicense` covers `cannabis|kratom`; `getRequiredSlots(plantType)`), so once the seed runs a kratom or general-herb filing is judged by the full cannabis กทล.1 attachment list.\n\n**6. `packages/validation/src/satisfaction-families.js:52` — the generic slot `other` satisfies a PDF-only legal requirement.**\n`land_rights` is declared `'PDF'` in `SLOT_ACCEPTED_TYPE`, but `other` (and `chanote`/`ns3`/`spk`) are absent from that table and fall to `DEFAULT_SLOT_TYPE = 'BOTH'`. The table is append-only by design, so the partial state cannot be edited back — only closed row by row, which is the situation the header says it wants to avoid.
